### Na Black Friday, o desafio não é apenas identificar o golpe, mas tomar a decisão segura

### Your Green Test Suite Proves Nothing Until You Break It

### Leio para 'desbestificar' as partes enferrujadas de privilégio masculino

## Papers

### Irreducible Governance Structure for Autonomous Agent Systems: Fair Allocation, Strategy-Proofness, and Multi-Scale Composition

Autonomous agent governance is commonly framed as a problem of enforcing correct execution. However, in multi-agent systems, an equally fundamental question precedes execution: which agents are allowed to act.

*Fontes: [cs.AI updates on arXiv.org](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31284>)*

### MOZAIC: An Audio-Video Deepfake Benchmark for Telling What Is Fake

Audio-video (AV) deepfakes can fake the face, the voice, or both. Most benchmarks only ask whether a clip is real or fake, so they cannot show whether a detector relies on the picture, the voice, or encoding traces.

*Fontes: [cs.AI updates on arXiv.org](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31301>)*

### Conservative Overlap Fuzzy Rough Sets for Incomplete Data

Attribute reduction in incomplete information systems is difficult because missing values break the equivalence-class structure of rough set theory. We propose the Conservative Overlap Fuzzy Rough Set (COFRS) model.

*Fontes: [cs.AI updates on arXiv.org](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31329>)*

### Beyond Static Charts: Benchmarking Interactive Chart-to-Code Generation for MLLMs

In recent years, multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved significant progress in chart-to-code generation.

*Fontes: [cs.AI updates on arXiv.org](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31345>)*

### What People Choose Not to Say: A Multimodal Benchmark for Fine-Grained Information Manipulation in Conversations

Recent advances in MLLMs have improved social understanding, enabling models to recognize emotions and distinguish truthful from deceptive behaviors.

*Fontes: [cs.AI updates on arXiv.org](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31346>)*

### Antares-Smaug: Adversarial Names, Tokens and Attack Rewrites: Sanitisers Miss Unseen Generalisations

Laya is an open decision model: given a text, a question and K named options with descriptions, it returns one probability per option in a single pass, and software may act on the answer unseen.

*Fontes: [cs.AI updates on arXiv.org](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31347>)*

### ContextLure: Exploiting Benign Conversation History for Indirect Prompt Injection

As LLMs become more capable, agents can perform more complex tasks with tools and maintain longer interactions with users.

*Fontes: [cs.AI updates on arXiv.org](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31424>)*

### Learning What vs. Learning How: Decomposing Task and Interface Competence in Agentic Training

LLM agents operate through harnesses that define system prompts, action formats, feedback loops, and resource constraints.

*Fontes: [cs.AI updates on arXiv.org](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31427>)*

### Taxi: Analyzing Agents at (Real) Scale

In this technical report, we present Taxi -- a system for debugging, clustering, and monitoring agents at scale. Taxi scales to millions of steps efficiently.

*Fontes: [cs.AI updates on arXiv.org](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31457>)*

### SpatialCraft: Evolving Procedural Intelligence from Experience and Skills for Spatial Reasoning Agents

Spatial reasoning agents increasingly rely on external tools, such as 3D reconstruction, segmentation, and geometric computation, to answer questions that a multimodal large language model (MLLM) cannot resolve from…

*Fontes: [cs.AI updates on arXiv.org](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31475>)*

### Evaluating Frontier-Model Agent Skills with Open-Weight Models in a Science Gateway

Scientific analyses that span many jobs and computing sites depend on workflow management systems to run reliably, but writing a production workflow requires expertise in such a system, which many domain scientists do…

*Fontes: [cs.AI updates on arXiv.org](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31480>)*

### XRL4T1D: A Post-hoc Explainability Framework for Reinforcement Learning in Type 1 Diabetes Management

Type 1 diabetes (T1D) requires lifelong insulin therapy, and reinforcement learning (RL) has shown promise for automated dosing in the UVA/Padova simulator.

*Fontes: [cs.AI updates on arXiv.org](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31498>)*

### PatientFlowSynth: Evaluating FHIR Agents with Matched Patient Charts

Agents that search patient charts must classify claims from retrieved records, including cases with missing or conflicting evidence.

*Fontes: [cs.AI updates on arXiv.org](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31521>)*

### One Step, Four Clocks: How GUI Agents Fall Out of Step

GUI agents can choose correct actions yet fail as interfaces change during deliberation. Latency alone cannot explain these failures because actions may become invalid at different interaction stages.

*Fontes: [cs.AI updates on arXiv.org](<https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31522>)*

### Agent Evaluation Without Ground Truth: A Null Proportion Perspective
